# State of Continental Trade, Highway & Canal Networks (`5.1e23` — `27` Spheres, `22` Canals, `118` Goods)

Generated by [gemini_trade_model.py](file:///home/antigravity/traviso761-debug/krator-master/tools/scale-model/gemini_trade_model.py) over live `5.1e23` Scale Model data (`tools/scale-model/model/data/` via [edit_522_ria_hydrology.py](file:///home/antigravity/traviso761-debug/krator-master/tools/scale-model/edit_522_ria_hydrology.py) and [edit_523_canals_and_settlements.py](file:///home/antigravity/traviso761-debug/krator-master/tools/scale-model/edit_523_canals_and_settlements.py)), [geology.json](file:///home/antigravity/traviso761-debug/krator-master/kits/resources/data/geology.json), and [net51-gemini.json](file:///home/antigravity/traviso761-debug/krator-master/openworld/net51-gemini.json) into [trade-gemini.json](file:///home/antigravity/traviso761-debug/krator-master/openworld/trade-gemini.json) and [roads-supplement-gemini.json](file:///home/antigravity/traviso761-debug/krator-master/openworld/roads-supplement-gemini.json).

> [!IMPORTANT]
> **27-Sphere Planetary Trade Summary (`Rulings #1–104` + `5.1e23` Canals)**: **118 trade & workshop I/O goods** (adding `clay`, `fireclay`, `ancient-biogel`, `inferior-biogel`, `superconductors`, `paktong`, and `nephrite-jade`) solved across **63 polities & autonomous enclaves** (`205` real on-map settlements) over the three-layer directed `4 km` Dijkstra multimodal graph (`Layer 0` Overland Highways & Trails + `Layer 1` Sea & Inland Lakes + `Layer 2` Directed Navigable Rivers & `22` Engineered Lock/Boat-Lift Canals) with `124` supplemental polity, Ria-Dynastic & frontier roads.
> - **Continental Engineered Canal Networks (`22` Canals, `3,178.2 km`, `203` Locks/Lifts)**: Explicit `DATA.canals` (`model/data/canals.json`) across **Eternal State (`8` bronze miter-lock canals)** uniting *Jìng-Hǎi* (`Mirror Sea`), `Līng-Kǒu`, `Fulcrum`, `Farport`, `Shuāng-Zhá`, `Wū-Xī`, `Lux`, `Hōng-Mā Wharf`, and `Palewharf`; **Ria Civilization (`7` Dynastic lock, boat-lift & summit-pound canals)** including `Mā-Kōu ("Iron Gates")`, `Tōng-Qū Three-Rivers Grand Canal`, `Kulūną`/`Hārta` Heartland loess trunks, non-ferrous `Līwa-Kōmpa` to `Lodestone`, `Sikwīngi` karst boat-lifts (`Springhollows` -> `Upper Ria`), and `Ākwala`; plus **Empire of Iziz (`2`)**, **Hykkousoi League (`2`)**, and **Kingdom of Voth (`3`)** (`4,070,783.7` cross-polity canal barge ton-km).
> - **`Ria Valley` Sinuous Dendritic Hydrology (`39` Rivers, `sin = 1.29–1.51`) & River-First vs. Medium-Highway Architecture**: Replaces raw `5.1`'s `8` ruler-straight channels (`sin = 1.02–1.12`) with **`39` stream-power-eroded dendritic rivers (`11,300 km` total channel length, mean sinuosity `1.389`)** and the **`x ≈ 702` `"Iron Gates"` hogback water-gap (`4,210 m³/s`)**. The Ria Dynasties maintain a **`0.72×` medium-grade overland highway network** between settlements (`5.8%` of in-basin inland ton-km for lateral feeder & `Weeping Valley` bypass links), while **`9101 Mā-Rīa` + Ria canals & lower `Pāk-Mā`/`Mō-Mā` forks carry `85.9%` of in-basin inland freight ton-km** as Ria's primary bulk highway!
> - **Twin Ria-Based Trans-National Organizations Pushing South ([NORTHERN-CARTELS-SCOPE.md](file:///home/antigravity/traviso761-debug/krator-master/core/region/NORTHERN-CARTELS-SCOPE.md), `Rulings #102–104`)**: Strictly neutral above both Ria Dynasties (`Springhollows` `1135,153`, `Mā-Kōu / "Iron Gates"` `702,182`, `Ria Sound` `626,140`), **(1) *The Pioneers / The Technicians*** (`PN-01..15`) export `Lodestone` `superconductors`, clean **hydro-dynamos** (`electrics`), silk-wrapped telegraph relays, and anomaly-proof flux-compasses (`lenses`) down twin southward pincers, rivaling southern Geomancer `oil`; **(2) *The Court of the Amber Seal*** ("The Seal-Bank", `AS-01..12`) acts as Krator's true trans-national sovereign bond, Ghost-Tsunami/*bottomry* cargo insurer, and customs/lock-farming bank.

## 0. `Ria Valley` Hydrology & River-vs-Highway Modal Split (`edit_522_ria_hydrology.py`)

| Metric | Value | Notes |
|:---|---:|:---|
| **Ria + Shroudhills Dendritic Rivers** | **`39`** (`+32` new 1st–3rd order branches) | Up from `7–8` ruler-straight lines; `11,299.8 km` total channel across `842,384 km²` (`13.41 km / 1,000 km²`) |
| **Channel Sinuosity (`9101 Mā-Rīa` / `9107 Pāk-Mā` / `9108 Mō-Mā`)** | **`1.290` / `1.368` / `1.380`** | `32` new tributaries span **`1.300 – 1.510`** (mean **`1.396`**) with stream-power valley erosion & meander benches |
| **`"Iron Gates"` Transverse Hogback Sill (`x ≈ 685–718`)** | **`+1,725 m` crest / `4,210 m³/s` gap** | `101.6 km` navigable water-gap (`slope = 0.00045`); puts `Heartland` (`580 mm`) & `Moleshelf` (`549 mm`) in `BSk` rain-shadow |
| **In-Basin Inland Modal Split: River & Canal Barges (`Layer 2`)** | **`85.9%` of inland ton-km** | `2,694,999.4` ton-km — **`Mā-Rīa`, `7` Ria Canals & lower `Pāk-Mā`/`Mō-Mā` are Ria's primary bulk highway** |
| **In-Basin Inland Modal Split: `0.72×` Ria Medium Highways** | **`5.8%` of inland ton-km** | `182,924.3` ton-km — inter-settlement post-roads, `Lodestone` feeder & `Moleshelf` bypass |
| **In-Basin Inland Modal Split: Frontier Pack/Caravan Trails** | **`8.2%` of inland ton-km** | `257,722.2` ton-km — `Spider Lake` / `Silkhead` & high mountain rim approaches |
